A manual locks:acquire on the same 4 paths succeeded at 04:34Z (lock d3bf79cb, coordination_domain /home/marsh-office/papercupai-workspace/papercusp), and the second Write was still denied. That message is the hook's 'stale operator rejects coordination_domain' branch (apps/operator/scripts/hooks/cc/__tests__/pretooluse-locks-decision.test.ts:618). So the operator the hook dials rejects the coordination_domain field while the MCP-served operator accepts it, which suggests the hook reaches a different (older) build than the papercusp-su MCP client. Effect: native Edit/Write is blocked for any session whose hook hits that operator. Workaround used: capability:write / capability:edit under the explicitly held lock. Record each entry from a real launched …","summary":"Baseline by observation. Freeze every role's, built-in identity's and worn stack's effective tool set in Avi's workspace, including each tool's argument scope. Record each entry from a real launched session's tool list or tool_invocations, with session ids and timestamps, never from the envelope code this plan changes. Enumerate the population (roles, built-in identities, and su in each mode and fleet position) from a source independent of that code, and record a count control. Also freeze su's vault behaviour on all four settings (grant terms, after-read holds, transcript visibility, recall) and a render of Avi's su prompt.
